scores.ndim != 2 or scores.shape[1] == 0:\n        return np.zeros_like(scores, dtype=int)\n    order = np.argsort(-scores, axis=1, kind=\"stable\")\n    ranks = np.empty_like(order)\n    rows = np.arange(scores.shape[0])[:, None]\n    ranks[rows, order] = np.arange(1, scores.shape[1] + 1)\n    return ranks\n\n\ndef rank_entropy(rank_samples_i: np.ndarray, n: int) -> float:\n    if n <= 1:\n np\n\n\ndef posterior_ranks(score_samples: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"score_samples [S, items] → ranks [S, items], 1 = best.\"\"\"\n    scores = np.asarray(score_samples, dtype=float)\n    if scores.ndim != 2 or scores.shape[1] == 0:\n        return np.zeros_like(scores, dtype=int)\n    order = np.argsort(-scores, axis=1, kind=\"stable\")\n    ranks = np.empty_like(order)\n    rows = np.arange(scores.shape[0])[:, None]\n    ranks[rows, order] = np.arange(1, scores.shape[1] + 1)\n    return ranks\n\n\ndef rank_entropy(rank_samples_i: np.ndarray, n: int) -> float:\n    if n <= 1:\n        return 0.0\n    counts = np.bincount(rank_samples_i.astype(int), minlength=n + 1)[1 : n + 1]\n    p = counts / max(1, counts.sum())\n    p = p[p > 0]\n    if p.size == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    return float(-(p * np.log(p)).sum())\n\n\ndef rank_summary(score_samples: np.ndarray, top_n: int) -> list[dict]:\n    scores = np.asarray(score_samples, dtype=float)\n    if scores.ndim != 2 or scores.shape[1] == 0:\n        return []\n    s_count, n = scores.shape\n    if s_count == 0:\n        return []\n    tn = max(1, min(int(top_n or n), n))\n    ranks = posterior_ranks(scores)\n    mean_scores = scores.mean(axis=0)\n    final_order = np.argsort(-mean_scores, kind=\"stable\")\n    assigned = np.empty(n, dtype=int)\n    assigned[final_order] = np.arange(1, n + 1)\n    log_n = float(np.log(n)) if n > 1 else 1.0\n    result = []\n    for i in range(n):\n        ri = ranks[:, i]\n        h = rank_entropy(ri, n)\n        result.append({\n            \"item\": i,\n            \"rank\": int(assigned[i]),\n            \"expected_rank\": float(ri.mean()),\n            \"median_rank\": float(np.median(ri)),\n            \"p_exact_rank\": float(np.mean(ri == assigned[i])),\n            \"p_best\": float(np.mean(ri == 1)),\n            \"p_top_n\": float(np.mean(ri <= tn)),\n            \"rank_ci95\": [\n                float(np.quantile(ri, 0.025)),\n                float(np.quantile(ri, 0.975)),\n            ],\n            \"rank_entropy\": h,\n            \"rank_entropy_norm\": float(h / log_n) if log_n > 0 else 0.0,\n        })\n    return result\n\n\ndef pairwise_win_matrix(\n    score_samples: np.ndarray,\n    kappa: np.ndarray | float = 1.0,\n    lapse: np.ndarray | float = 0.0,\n) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Posterior P(i>j). Diagonal is 0.5.\"\"\"\n    scores = np.asarray(score_samples, dtype=float)\n    if scores.ndim != 2 or scores.shape[1] == 0:\n        return np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=float)\n    s_count, n = scores.shape\n    if s_count == 0:\n        return np.full((n, n), 0.5)\n    kap = np.asarray(kappa, dtype=float)\n    if kap.ndim == 0:\n        kap = np.full(s_count, float(kap))\n    lam = np.asarray(lapse, dtype=float)\n    if lam.ndim == 0:\n        lam = np.full(s_count, float(lam))\n    kap = kap.reshape(s_count, 1, 1)\n    lam = lam.reshape(s_count, 1, 1)\n    diff = scores[:, :, None] - scores[:, None, :]\n    p = (1.0 - lam) * _expit(kap * diff) + 0.5 * lam\n    p_mean = p.mean(axis=0)\n    np.fill_diagonal(p_mean, 0.5)\n    return p_mean\n\n\ndef pairwise_q_from_p(p_mat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    n = p_mat.shape[0]\n    if n <= 1:\n        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)\n    out = (p_mat.sum(axis=1) - np.diag(p_mat)) / float(n - 1)\n    return np.clip(out, 0.0, 1.0)\n\n\ndef q_per_draw(\n    score_samples: np.ndarray,\n    kappa: np.ndarray | float = 1.0,\n    lapse: np.ndarray | float = 0.0,\n) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Q_i for every draw. Shape [S, items].\"\"\"\n    scores = np.asarray(score_samples, dtype=float)\n    if scores.ndim != 2 or scores.shape[0] == 0 or scores.shape[1] == 0:\n        return np.zeros_like(scores, dtype=float)\n    s_count, n = scores.shape\n    if n <= 1:\n        return np.ones((s_count, n), dtype=float)\n    kap = np.asarray(kappa, dtype=float)\n    if kap.ndim == 0:\n        kap = np.full(s_count, float(kap))\n    lam = np.asarray(lapse, dtype=float)\n    if lam.ndim == 0:\n        lam = np.full(s_count, float(lam))\n    kap = kap.reshape(s_count, 1, 1)\n    lam = lam.reshape(s_count, 1, 1)\n    diff = scores[:, :, None] - scores[:, None, :]\n    p = (1.0 - lam) * _expit(kap * diff) + 0.5 * lam\n    eye = np.eye(n, dtype=bool)\n    p[:, eye] = np.nan\n    return np.nanmean(p, axis=2)\n\n\ndef _expit(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)))","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":128,"totalLines":128,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/utils/bt_inference/ranks.py","time":{"start":1787325172493,"end":1787325173023}},"id":"prt_024e1caee001nyMB7m77yucdsl","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_024e1a79f001YDAuWb0LZav21a"}}

    order = np.argsort(-mean_s, kind=\"stable\")\n            ranks = np.empty_like(order)\n            ranks[order] = np.arange(1, order.size + 1)\n            for local, iid in enumerate(fit.item_ids):\n                personal_ranks.setdefault(int(iid), []).append(float(ranks[local]))\n    for row in option_ranking:\n        vals = personal_ranks.get(int(row[\"id\"])) or []\n        if len(vals) >= 2:\n            sd = float(np.std(vals))\n            row[\"participant_rank_sd\"] = sd\n            n = max(1, len(alt_ids) - 1)\n            row[\"polarizing\"] = sd > 0.35 * n\n        else:\n            row[\"participant_rank_sd\"] = 0.0\n            row[\"polarizing\"] = False\n\n\ndef _fit_personal_channel(obs: list[PairObs], allowed: set[int], n_samples: int, seed: int) -> FitResult | None:\n    data = prepare_channel(obs, allowed)\n    if data is None:\n        return None\n    data.use_hierarchy = False\n    return fit_channel(data, n_samples=max(80, min(n_samples, 240)), seed=seed)\n\n\ndef _personal_bundles(\n    *,\n    alts,\n    facs,\n    obs: maxCompleteView.value = false\n  lastFlushedCount.value = 0\n  if (multiFactorMode.value) {\n    pendingGroup.value = g\n    showGroupIntro.value = true\n    runner.stop()\n  } else {\n res.project_settings || {}\n  const g = res.next_group as SortGroupPayload | null | undefined\n\n  if (reachedMaxRecommended(res)) {\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    pendingGroup.value = null\n null\n    showGroupIntro.value = false\n    runner.stop()\n  }\n}\n\nfunction storeCredentials(form: Record<string, unknown>) {\n  gateCreds.value = mergeShareGateCreds(gateCreds.value, form, applyPayload(res)\n    if (res.personal_vote?.is_complete && shareKind.value === 'vote_view') {\n      completeView.value = true\n      votesCaptured.value = Number(res.votes_captured || {\n    gateLoading.value = false\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function submitCompletedGroup() {\n  const pkg = runner.buildPackage()\n  if (!pkg) return\n  fetching.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await shares.extCompleteGroup(token.value, {\n      ...gateCreds.value,\n      share_kind: shareKind.value,\n      prior_group: pkg,\n    }) as any\n    applyPayload(res)\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    error.value = e?.message || 'Failed to save group'\n  } finally {\n    fetching.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nwatch(\n  () => runner.complete.value,\n  async (done) => {\n    if (done && runner.group.value) await submitCompletedGroup()\n  },\n)\n\nconst lastFlushedCount = ref(0)\nlet idleTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null\n\nfunction flushThresholds() {\n  return {\n    count: Math.max(1, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_flush_count || 5)),\n    idleMs: Math.max(5, Number(projectSettings.value.partial_idle_seconds || 90)) * 1000,\n  }\n}\n\nasync function lastFlushedCount.value) return\n  try {\n    await shares.extSaveGroup(token.value, {\n      ...gateCreds.value,\n      share_kind: shareKind.value,\n      prior_group: pkg,\n    })\n runner.complete.value) return\n    const { count, idleMs } = flushThresholds()\n    if (n - lastFlushedCount.value >= count) void flushPartial()\n    idleTimer = setTimeout(() => { void flushPartial() }, idleMs)\n  },\n)\n\nonBeforeUnmount(() => {\n  if (idleTimer) clearTimeout(idleTimer)\n})\n\nfunction onPause() {\n  paused.value = true\n  runner.setPaused(true)\n}\nfunction onResume() {\n  paused.value = false\n  runner.setPaused(false)\n}\n\nasync function continuePastMax() {\n  maxCompleteAcknowledged.value = true\n  maxCompleteView.value = false\n  const held = heldNextGroup.value\n ParticipantInfluenceMode[] = [\n10:   'comparisons',\n11:   'balanced',\n12:   'participants_normalized',\n13: ]\n14: \n15: export const PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_BETA: Record<ParticipantInfluenceMode, INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS_FLOOR = 3\n23: export const IDLE_FLUSH_MS = 90_000\n24: \n25: export type CoherenceMethod = 'spearman' | 'kendall'\n26: \n27: /** Minimal settings retained for local prefs / influence labels (no probe SoftMax). */\n28: export interface RankingSettings {\n29:   coherenceMethod: CoherenceMethod\n30: }\n31: \n32: export const defaultRankingSettings = (): RankingSettings => ({\n33:   coherenceMethod: 'spearman',\n34: })\n35: \n36: export interface RankItem {\n37:   id: number\n38:   title: string\n39:   description?: string | null\n40:   active?: boolean\n41: }\n42: \n43: // Legacy type aliases kept so older imports compile during transition.\n44: export type ObservationResponse = 'winner' | 'tie' | 'unsure' | 'skipped'\n45: export type ObservationType = number\n57:   _weight?: number\n58: }\n59: \n60: /** Minimal probe shape still referenced by vote-batch typing (sort UI owns real probes). */\n61: export interface ProbeCandidate {\n62:   type: ObservationType\n63:   items: RankItem[]\n64:   criterion: RankItem | null\n65:   probability?: number\n66:   predictedId: number\n67:   predictedProbability?: number\n68:   informationScore: number\n69:   scenarioId?: string\n70: }\n71: \n72: export function normalizeParticipantInfluenceMode(mode?: string | null): ParticipantInfluenceMode {\n73:   const m = String(mode || 'comparisons').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[-\\s]/g, '_')\n74:   if (m === 'comparison' || m === 'equal_comparisons' || m === 'comparisons_equal') return 'comparisons'\n75:   if (m === 'participant' || m === 'participants' || m === 'normalized' || m === 'participant_normalized') {\n76:     return 'participants_normalized'\n77:   }\n78:   if ((PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_MODES as string[]).includes(m)) return m as ParticipantInfluenceMode\n79:   return 'comparisons'\n80: }\n81: \n82: export function clampInfluenceMinComparisons(value?: number | null): number {\n83:   const n = Math.round(Number(value ?? DEFAULT_INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS))\n84:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return DEFAULT_INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS\n85:   return Math.max(INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS_FLOOR, n)\n86: }\n87: \n88: export function clamp(value: number, lo = 0, hi = 1) {\n89:   return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, value))\n90: }\n91: \n92: /** Convert API metric (0–1 fraction or already 0–100) to integer percent points. */\n93: export function asMetricPercent(value: unknown): number {\n94:   const n = Number(value ?? 0)\n95:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0\n96:   if (n <= 1) return Math.round(n * 100)\n97:   return Math.round(n)\n98: }\n99: \n100: /** Strong / Solid / … labels. Accepts 0–1 or 0–100. */\n101: export function metricLabel(value: number) {\n102:   const v = asMetricPercent(value)\n103:   if (v >= 80) return 'Strong'\n104:   if (v >= 60) return 'Solid'\n105:   if (v >= 40) return 'Building'\n106:   if (v >= 20) return 'Early'\n107:   return 'Sparse'\n108: }\n109: \n110: /**\n111:  * Format a ranking score as percent.\n112:  * Server scores are normalized preference in [0, 1].\n113:  * Values already > 1 are treated as percent points (defensive).\n114:  */\n115: export function formatPercent(score: number) {\n116:   const n = Number(score)\n117:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0%'\n118:   if (n <= 1) return `${Math.round(n * 100)}%`\n119:   return `${Math.round(n)}%`\n120: }\n121: \n122: /** Bar width 0–100 from a 0–1 (or 0–100) score. */\n123: export function scoreBarPercent(score: number) {\n124:   const n = Number(score)\n125:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0\n126:   if (n <= 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n * 100))\n127:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n))\n128: }\n129: \n130: /** Lead between two 0–1 scores as integer \"points\" (percent points). */\n131: export function scoreLeadPoints(a: number, b: number) {\n132:   return Math.round(Math.abs(scoreBarPercent(a) - scoreBarPercent(b)))\n133: }\n134: \n135: export function effectiveCriteria(criteria: RankItem[]): RankItem[] {\n136:   const active = (criteria || []).filter(c => c.active !== false)\n137:   if (active.length) return active\n138:   return [{ id: 0, title: 'Overall' }]\n139: }\n140: \n141: export function mean(values: number[]) {\n142:   if (!values.length) return 0\n143:   return values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length\n144: }\n145: \n146: export type RankingMode = 'find_best' | 'find_top_3' | 'find_top_half' | 'rank_all'\n147: \n148: export function normalizeRankingMode(\n149:   mode?: string | null,\n150:   exclusive?: boolean | null,\n151: ): RankingMode {\n152:   const m = String(mode || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[-\\s]/g, '_')\n153:   if (m === 'find_best' || m === 'winner' || m === 'pick_one' || m === 'exclusive') return 'find_best'\n154:   if (m === 'find_top_3' || m === 'top_3' || m === 'top3') return 'find_top_3'\n155:   if (m === 'find_top_half' || m === 'top_half') return 'find_top_half'\n156:   if (m === 'rank_all' || m === 'full' || m === 'rank') return 'rank_all'\n157:   return exclusive ? 'find_best' : 'rank_all'\n158: }\n159: \n160: export function headlineOptionLabel(mode: RankingMode) {\n161:   return mode === 'find_best' ? 'Recommended option' : 'Top option'\n162: }\n163: \n164: function finiteNumber(value: unknown): number | null {\n165:   if (value == null || value === '') return null\n166:   const n = Number(value)\n167:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null\n168: }\n169: \n170: /** Null when missing; treat exact 0 as empty when that value cannot be real. */\n171: export function nullableUnit(value: unknown, treatZeroAsEmpty = false): number | null {\n172:   const n = finiteNumber(value)\n173:   if (n == null) return null\n174:   if (treatZeroAsEmpty && n === 0) return null\n175:   return n\n176: }\n177: \n178: export function formatNullablePercent(value: unknown, treatZeroAsEmpty = false) {\n179:   const n = nullableUnit(value, treatZeroAsEmpty)\n180:   if (n == null) return '—'\n181:   return formatPercent(n)\n182: }\n183: \n184: /** 1-based rank_ci95 → \"rank 2\" or \"ranks 1–3\". */\n185: export function formatRankCi(ci: unknown): string | null {\n186:   if (!Array.isArray(ci) || ci.length < 2) return null\n187:   const lo = finiteNumber(ci[0])\n188:   const hi = finiteNumber(ci[1])\n189:   if (lo == null || hi == null) return null\n190:   const a = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.min(lo, hi)))\n191:   const b = Math.max(a, Math.round(Math.max(lo, hi)))\n192:   if (a === b) return `rank ${a}`\n193:   return `ranks ${a}–${b}`\n194: }\n195: \n196: export function formatWeightCi(ci: unknown): string | null {\n197:   if (!Array.isArray(ci) || ci.length < 2) return null\n198:   const lo = finiteNumber(ci[0])\n199:   const hi = finiteNumber(ci[1])\n200:   if (lo == null || hi == null) return null\n201:   return `${formatPercent(lo)}–${formatPercent(hi)}`\n202: }\n203: \n204: export function formatExpectedRank(value: unknown): string | null {\n205:   const n = finiteNumber(value)\n206:   if (n == null) return null\n207:   const rounded = Math.round(n * 10) / 10\n208:   return Number.isInteger(rounded) ? String(rounded) : rounded.toFixed(1)\n209: }\n210: \n211: /** p_best in find_best; p_top_n in top-N modes; omit for rank_all. */\n212: export function optionChanceCaption(\n213:   row: { p_best?: unknown, p_top_n?: unknown } | null | undefined,\n214:   mode: RankingMode,\n215:   topN?: number | null,\n216: ): string | null {\n217:   if (!row) return null\n218:   if (mode === 'find_best') {\n219:     const p = nullableUnit(row.p_best)\n220:     if (p == null) return null\n221:     return `${formatPercent(p)} chance it's #1`\n222:   }\n223:   if (mode === 'find_top_3' || mode === 'find_top_half') {\n224:     const p = nullableUnit(row.p_top_n)\n225:     if (p == null) return null\n226:     const n = Math.max(1, finiteNumber(topN) || (mode === 'find_top_3' ? 3 : 1))\n227:     return `${formatPercent(p)} chance in top ${n}`\n228:   }\n229:   return null\n230: }\n231: \n232: export function polarizingCaption(row: {\n233:   polarizing?: unknown\n234:   participant_rank_sd?: unknown\n235:   title?: string\n236:   expected_rank?: unknown\n237:   rank?: unknown\n238: } | null | undefined): string | null {\n239:   if (!row) return null\n240:   const sd = finiteNumber(row.participant_rank_sd)\n241:   const flagged = row.polarizing === true || (sd != null && sd > 1.2)\n242:   if (!flagged) return null\n243:   const er = finiteNumber(row.expected_rank) ?? (finiteNumber(row.rank) != null ? Number(row.rank) + 1 : null)\n244:   const rankLabel = er != null ? String(Math.round(er)) : '?'\n245:   const title = String(row.title || 'This option')\n246:   return `${title}: consensus #${rankLabel}, highly polarizing`\n247: }\n248: \n249: export interface InsightChip {\n250:   key: 'agreement' | 'repeatability' | 'stability'\n251:   label: string\n252:   display: string\n253:   caption: string\n254:   empty: boolean\n255: }\n256: \n257: /**\n258:  * Primary Results chips. Agreement = expected pair agreement.\n259:  * Repeatability / Stability use posterior same-winner vs same-top-N;\n260:  * rank_all does not use same-top-N (it is always 1 when top_n = n).\n261:  */\n262: export function insightMetricChips(report: Record<string, any> | null | undefined): InsightChip[] {\n263:   const mode = normalizeRankingMode(report?.ranking_mode, report?.exclusive_mode)\n264:   const coherence = report?.coherence && typeof report.coherence === 'object' ? report.coherence : {}\n265:   const stab = report?.posterior_stability && typeof report.posterior_stability === 'object'\n266:     ? report.posterior_stability\n267:     : {}\n268:   const comparisons = finiteNumber(report?.comparison_count ?? report?.total_observations) ?? 0\n269:   const hasData = comparisons > 0\n270:   const topN = Math.max(1, finiteNumber(report?.top_n) || 1)\n271:   const nOptions = Array.isArray(report?.option_ranking) ? report.option_ranking.length : 0\n272:   const topNInformative = nOptions > 1 && topN < nOptions\n273: \n274:   const agreement = nullableUnit(coherence.expected_pair_agreement, true)\n275:   const sameWinner = nullableUnit(stab.same_winner_repeat_probability, !hasData)\n276:   const sameTopN = nullableUnit(stab.same_top_n_repeat_probability, !hasData)\n277: \n278:   const useTopN = (mode === 'find_top_3' || mode === 'find_top_half') && topNInformative\n279:   const repeatability = useTopN ? sameTopN : sameWinner\n280:   const stability = useTopN ? sameWinner : (topNInformative ? sameTopN : sameWinner)\n281: \n282:   const repeatCaption = useTopN\n283:     ? `Same top ${topN} if these participants compared again`\n284:     : 'Same winner if these participants compared again'\n285:   const stabilityCaption = useTopN\n286:     ? 'Same winner with a similar new group'\n287:     : 'Same result with a similar new group'\n288: \n289:   return [\n290:     {\n291:       key: 'agreement',\n292:       label: 'Agreement',\n293:       display: agreement == null ? '—' : formatPercent(agreement),\n294:       caption: agreement == null\n295:         ? 'Not enough comparisons'\n296:         : 'How often participants would pick the same pair the same way',\n297:       empty: agreement == null,\n298:     },\n299:     {\n300:       key: 'repeatability',\n301:       label: 'Repeatability',\n302:       display: repeatability == null ? '—' : formatPercent(repeatability),\n303:       caption: repeatability == null ? 'Not enough comparisons' : repeatCaption,\n304:       empty: repeatability == null,\n305:     },\n306:     {\n307:       key: 'stability',\n308:       label: 'Stability',\n309:       display: stability == null ? '—' : formatPercent(stability),\n310:       caption: stability == null ? 'Not enough comparisons' : stabilityCaption,\n311:       empty: stability == null,\n312:     },\n313:   ]\n314: }\n315: \n316: /** Map server ranking rows into a stable UI shape (score always 0–1). */\n317: export function mapServerRanking(rows: any[] | undefined | null) {\n318:   if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) return null\n319:   const n = rows.length\n320:   return rows.map((r, i) => {\n321:     // null mean_rank = option has no rank data in this slice (never compared under\n322:     // the factor); keep it null so the UI shows an explicit \"no data\" row.\n323:     const meanRank = r.mean_rank != null ? Number(r.mean_rank) : null\n324:     let score = r.score != null ? Number(r.score) : NaN\n325:     // Legacy rank-distance scores were in [0, n−1]\n326:     if (Number.isFinite(score) && score > 1 && n > 1) {\n327:       score = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, score / (n - 1)))\n328:     }\n329:     if (!Number.isFinite(score)) {\n330:       score = meanRank != null && n > 1 ? Math.max(0, (n - 1 - meanRank) / (n - 1)) : 0\n331:     }\n332:     const weight = r.normalizedWeight ?? r.weight ?? r.normalized_weight\n333:     const rankCi = Array.isArray(r.rank_ci95) ? r.rank_ci95.map(Number) : undefined\n334:     const weightCi = Array.isArray(r.weight_ci95) ? r.weight_ci95.map(Number) : undefined\n335:     return {\n336:       id: r.id,\n337:       title: r.title,\n338:       description: r.description,\n339:       score,\n340:       mean_rank: meanRank,\n341:       rank: r.rank ?? i,\n342:       normalizedWeight: weight != null ? Number(weight) : undefined,\n343:       weight: weight != null ? Number(weight) : undefined,\n344:       confidence: r.confidence,\n345:       stability: r.stability,\n346:       pass_consistency: r.pass_consistency,\n347:       leader: r.leader,\n348:       data_points: r.data_points ?? r.evidence ?? 0,\n349:       evidence: r.evidence ?? r.data_points ?? 0,\n350:       expected_rank: finiteNumber(r.expected_rank),\n351:       median_rank: finiteNumber(r.median_rank),\n352:       p_best: finiteNumber(r.p_best),\n353:       p_top_n: finiteNumber(r.p_top_n),\n354:       p_exact_rank: finiteNumber(r.p_exact_rank),\n355:       rank_ci95: rankCi?.length === 2 && rankCi.every(Number.isFinite) ? rankCi : undefined,\n356:       rank_entropy: finiteNumber(r.rank_entropy),\n357:       pairwise_win_strength: finiteNumber(r.pairwise_win_strength),\n358:       participant_rank_sd: finiteNumber(r.participant_rank_sd),\n359:       polarizing: r.polarizing === true,\n360:       weight_ci95: weightCi?.length === 2 && weightCi.every(Number.isFinite) ? weightCi : undefined,\n361:       discrimination: finiteNumber(r.discrimination),\n362:       leverage: finiteNumber(r.leverage),\n363:       p_most_important: finiteNumber(r.p_most_important),\n364:     }\n365:   })\n366: }\n367: \n368: export interface PersonalSubmitterRankings {\n369:   options: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof mapServerRanking>>\n370:   factors: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof mapServerRanking>>\n371:   rankingMode: RankingMode\n372:   topN: number\n373:   confidence: number | null\n374:   showFactors: boolean\n375: }\n376: \n377: export function personalRankingsFromSummary(\n378:   summary: Record<string, any> | null | undefined,\n379:   projectSettings?: Record<string, unknown> | null,\n380: ): PersonalSubmitterRankings {\n381:   const options = mapServerRanking(summary?.alternative_leaderboard) || []\n382:   const factors = mapServerRanking(summary?.factor_leaderboard) || []\n383:   const rankingMode = normalizeRankingMode(\n384:     (projectSettings?.ranking_mode ?? summary?.ranking_mode) as string | undefined,\n385:     (projectSettings?.project_exclusive_mode ?? summary?.exclusive_mode) as boolean | undefined,\n386:   )\n387:   const fromSummary = finiteNumber(summary?.top_n)\n388:   const topN = Math.max(\n389:     1,\n390:     fromSummary ?? (rankingMode === 'find_top_3' ? 3 : 1),\n391:   )\n392:   return {\n393:     options,\n394:     factors,\n395:     rankingMode,\n396:     topN,\n397:     confidence: finiteNumber(summary?.confidence),\n398:     showFactors: factors.length > 1,\n399:   }\n400: }\n401: \n402: /** JSON often stringifies numeric map keys; tolerate number | string | null. */\n403: export function lookupRankMap<T>(\n404:   maps: Record<string | number, T> | null | undefined,\n405:   key: number | string | null | undefined,\n406: ): T | undefined {\n407:   if (!maps || key === undefined) return undefined\n408:   const rec = maps as Record<string | number, T>\n409:   if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, key as PropertyKey)) return rec[key as keyof typeof rec]\n410:   if (key === null) {\n411:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, 'null')) return rec.null as T\n412:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, 0)) return rec[0]\n413:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, '0')) return rec['0']\n414:     return undefined\n415:   }\n416:   const s = String(key)\n417:   if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, s)) return rec[s]\n418:   const n = Number(key)\n419:   if (Number.isFinite(n) && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, n)) return rec[n]\n420:   return undefined\n421: }\n422: \n423: /**\n424:  * Build a sorted ranking from a mean-rank map (lower mean_rank = better).\n425:  * Used for per-participant per-factor #1s when only option_ranks_by_factor is present.\n426:  */\n427: export function rankingFromMeanRankMap(\n428:   ranksMap: Record<string | number, number> | null | undefined,\n429:   items: RankItem[],\n430: ): Array<{ id: number, title: string, mean_rank: number, rank: number, score: number }> {\n431:   if (!ranksMap || !items?.length) return []\n432:   const rows = items\n433:     .map((item) => {\n434:       const raw = lookupRankMap(ranksMap, item.id)\n435:       if (raw == null) return null\n436:       const meanRank = Number(raw)\n437:       if (!Number.isFinite(meanRank)) return null\n438:       return { id: item.id, title: item.title, mean_rank: meanRank }\n439:     })\n440:     .filter((r): r is { id: number, title: string, mean_rank: number } => r != null)\n441:   rows.sort((a, b) => a.mean_rank - b.mean_rank || a.id - b.id)\n442:   const n = rows.length\n443:   return rows.map((r, i) => ({\n444:     ...r,\n445:     rank: i,\n446:     score: n > 1 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (n - 1 - r.mean_rank) / (n - 1))) : 1,\n447:   }))\n448: }\n449: \n450: /**\n451:  * All options tied for best in a ranking (shared best mean_rank, else shared best score).\n452:  * Ranking lists break remaining ties by id for display order — concentration must not.\n453:  */\n454: export function tiedLeadersFromRanking(\n455:   ranking: Array<{ id?: number, mean_rank?: number, score?: number, title?: string }> | null | undefined,\n456: ): Array<{ id: number, title: string }> {\n457:   if (!ranking?.length) return []\n458:   const rows = ranking\n459:     .map(r => ({\n460:       id: Number(r.id),\n461:       title: String(r.title || ''),\n462:       mean_rank: r.mean_rank != null ? Number(r.mean_rank) : NaN,\n463:       score: r.score != null ? Number(r.score) : NaN,\n464:     }))\n465:     .filter(r => Number.isFinite(r.id))\n466:   if (!rows.length) return []\n467: \n468:   const withMean = rows.filter(r => Number.isFinite(r.mean_rank))\n469:   if (withMean.length) {\n470:     const best = Math.min(...withMean.map(r => r.mean_rank))\n471:     return withMean\n472:       .filter(r => Math.abs(r.mean_rank - best) < 1e-9)\n473:       .map(r => ({ id: r.id, title: r.title }))\n474:   }\n475: \n476:   const withScore = rows.filter(r => Number.isFinite(r.score))\n477:   if (!withScore.length) return [{ id: rows[0].id, title: rows[0].title }]\n478:   const bestScore = Math.max(...withScore.map(r => r.score))\n479:   return withScore\n480:     .filter(r => Math.abs(r.score - bestScore) < 1e-9)\n481:     .map(r => ({ id: r.id, title: r.title }))\n482: }\n483: \n484: /** #1 option(s) under a factor for one participant row from the report. */\n485: export function participantLeadersUnderFactor(\n486:   participant: any,\n487:   factorId: number | null | undefined,\n488:   items: RankItem[],\n489: ): Array<{ id: number, title: string }> {\n490:   if (!participant) return []\n491:   if (factorId == null) {\n492:     const ranking = mapServerRanking(\n493:       participant.option_ranking || participant.ranking,\n494:     ) || []\n495:     if (ranking.length) return tiedLeadersFromRanking(ranking)\n496:     const leader = participant.leader\n497:     if (leader && typeof leader === 'object' && leader.id != null) {\n498:       return [{ id: Number(leader.id), title: String(leader.title || '') }]\n499:     }\n500:     return []\n501:   }\n502:   const ranks = lookupRankMap(participant.option_ranks_by_factor, factorId)\n503:   const ranking = rankingFromMeanRankMap(ranks as Record<string | number, number> | undefined, items)\n504:   return tiedLeadersFromRanking(ranking)\n505: }\n506: \n507: /** First #1 under a factor (display convenience). */\n508: export function participantLeaderUnderFactor(\n509:   participant: any,\n510:   factorId: number | null | undefined,\n511:   items: RankItem[],\n512: ): { id: number, title: string } | null {\n513:   return participantLeadersUnderFactor(participant, factorId, items)[0] || null\n514: }\n515: \n516: export function mergeRankingSettings(\n517:   ...sources: Array<Partial<RankingSettings> | Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined>\n518: ): RankingSettings {\n519:   const base = defaultRankingSettings()\n520:   for (const src of sources) {\n521:     if (!src) continue\n522:     const s = src as Record<string, unknown>\n523:     if (s.coherenceMethod != null || s.coherence_method != null) {\n524:       const m = String(s.coherenceMethod ?? s.coherence_method).toLowerCase()\n525:       base.coherenceMethod = m === 'kendall' ? 'kendall' : 'spearman'\n526:     }\n527:   }\n528:   return base\n529: }\n\n(End of file - total 529 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"/**\n * Display helpers and light client settings for ranked-group Results.\n * Pairwise Bayesian ranking has been removed — all Results math lives on the server\n * (src/utils/vote_ranking.py). See algos.md.\n */\n\nexport type ParticipantInfluenceMode = 'comparisons' | 'balanced' | 'participants_normalized'\n\nexport const PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_MODES: ParticipantInfluenceMode[] = [\n  'comparisons',\n  'balanced',\n  'participants_normalized',\n]\n\nexport const PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_BETA: Record<ParticipantInfluenceMode, number> = {\n  comparisons: 1,\n  balanced: 0.5,\n  participants_normalized: 0.1,\n}\n","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/utils/ranking.ts","text":"/**\n * Display helpers and light client settings for ranked-group Results.\n * Pairwise Bayesian ranking has been removed — all Results math lives on the server\n * (src/utils/vote_ranking.py). See algos.md.\n */\n\nexport type ParticipantInfluenceMode = 'comparisons' | 'balanced' | 'participants_normalized'\n\nexport const PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_MODES: ParticipantInfluenceMode[] = [\n  'comparisons',\n  'balanced',\n  'participants_normalized',\n]\n\nexport const PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_BETA: Record<ParticipantInfluenceMode, number> = {\n  comparisons: 1,\n  balanced: 0.5,\n  participants_normalized: 0.1,\n}\n\nexport const DEFAULT_INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS = 10\nexport const INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS_FLOOR = 3\nexport const IDLE_FLUSH_MS = 90_000\n\nexport type CoherenceMethod = 'spearman' | 'kendall'\n\n/** Minimal settings retained for local prefs / influence labels (no probe SoftMax). */\nexport interface RankingSettings {\n  coherenceMethod: CoherenceMethod\n}\n\nexport const defaultRankingSettings = (): RankingSettings => ({\n  coherenceMethod: 'spearman',\n})\n\nexport interface RankItem {\n  id: number\n  title: string\n  description?: string | null\n  active?: boolean\n}\n\n// Legacy type aliases kept so older imports compile during transition.\nexport type ObservationResponse = 'winner' | 'tie' | 'unsure' | 'skipped'\nexport type ObservationType = 'alternative' | 'criteria'\nexport interface Observation {\n  id?: number | string\n  type: ObservationType\n  criterionId: number | null\n  itemIds: number[]\n  response: ObservationResponse\n  winnerId?: number | null\n  predictedId?: number | null\n  predictedProbability?: number | null\n  predictionCorrect?: boolean | null\n  participant_id?: number\n  _weight?: number\n}\n\n/** Minimal probe shape still referenced by vote-batch typing (sort UI owns real probes). */\nexport interface ProbeCandidate {\n  type: ObservationType\n  items: RankItem[]\n  criterion: RankItem | null\n  probability?: number\n  predictedId: number\n  predictedProbability?: number\n  informationScore: number\n  scenarioId?: string\n}\n\nexport function normalizeParticipantInfluenceMode(mode?: string | null): ParticipantInfluenceMode {\n  const m = String(mode || 'comparisons').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[-\\s]/g, '_')\n  if (m === 'comparison' || m === 'equal_comparisons' || m === 'comparisons_equal') return 'comparisons'\n  if (m === 'participant' || m === 'participants' || m === 'normalized' || m === 'participant_normalized') {\n    return 'participants_normalized'\n  }\n  if ((PARTICIPANT_INFLUENCE_MODES as string[]).includes(m)) return m as ParticipantInfluenceMode\n  return 'comparisons'\n}\n\nexport function clampInfluenceMinComparisons(value?: number | null): number {\n  const n = Math.round(Number(value ?? DEFAULT_INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS))\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return DEFAULT_INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS\n  return Math.max(INFLUENCE_MIN_COMPARISONS_FLOOR, n)\n}\n\nexport function clamp(value: number, lo = 0, hi = 1) {\n  return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, value))\n}\n\n/** Convert API metric (0–1 fraction or already 0–100) to integer percent points. */\nexport function asMetricPercent(value: unknown): number {\n  const n = Number(value ?? 0)\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0\n  if (n <= 1) return Math.round(n * 100)\n  return Math.round(n)\n}\n\n/** Strong / Solid / … labels. Accepts 0–1 or 0–100. */\nexport function metricLabel(value: number) {\n  const v = asMetricPercent(value)\n  if (v >= 80) return 'Strong'\n  if (v >= 60) return 'Solid'\n  if (v >= 40) return 'Building'\n  if (v >= 20) return 'Early'\n  return 'Sparse'\n}\n\n/**\n * Format a ranking score as percent.\n * Server scores are normalized preference in [0, 1].\n * Values already > 1 are treated as percent points (defensive).\n */\nexport function formatPercent(score: number) {\n  const n = Number(score)\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return '0%'\n  if (n <= 1) return `${Math.round(n * 100)}%`\n  return `${Math.round(n)}%`\n}\n\n/** Bar width 0–100 from a 0–1 (or 0–100) score. */\nexport function scoreBarPercent(score: number) {\n  const n = Number(score)\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0\n  if (n <= 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n * 100))\n  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n))\n}\n\n/** Lead between two 0–1 scores as integer \"points\" (percent points). */\nexport function scoreLeadPoints(a: number, b: number) {\n  return Math.round(Math.abs(scoreBarPercent(a) - scoreBarPercent(b)))\n}\n\nexport function effectiveCriteria(criteria: RankItem[]): RankItem[] {\n  const active = (criteria || []).filter(c => c.active !== false)\n  if (active.length) return active\n  return [{ id: 0, title: 'Overall' }]\n}\n\nexport function mean(values: number[]) {\n  if (!values.length) return 0\n  return values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length\n}\n\nexport type RankingMode = 'find_best' | 'find_top_3' | 'find_top_half' | 'rank_all'\n\nexport function normalizeRankingMode(\n  mode?: string | null,\n  exclusive?: boolean | null,\n): RankingMode {\n  const m = String(mode || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[-\\s]/g, '_')\n  if (m === 'find_best' || m === 'winner' || m === 'pick_one' || m === 'exclusive') return 'find_best'\n  if (m === 'find_top_3' || m === 'top_3' || m === 'top3') return 'find_top_3'\n  if (m === 'find_top_half' || m === 'top_half') return 'find_top_half'\n  if (m === 'rank_all' || m === 'full' || m === 'rank') return 'rank_all'\n  return exclusive ? 'find_best' : 'rank_all'\n}\n\nexport function headlineOptionLabel(mode: RankingMode) {\n  return mode === 'find_best' ? 'Recommended option' : 'Top option'\n}\n\nfunction finiteNumber(value: unknown): number | null {\n  if (value == null || value === '') return null\n  const n = Number(value)\n  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null\n}\n\n/** Null when missing; treat exact 0 as empty when that value cannot be real. */\nexport function nullableUnit(value: unknown, treatZeroAsEmpty = false): number | null {\n  const n = finiteNumber(value)\n  if (n == null) return null\n  if (treatZeroAsEmpty && n === 0) return null\n  return n\n}\n\nexport function formatNullablePercent(value: unknown, treatZeroAsEmpty = false) {\n  const n = nullableUnit(value, treatZeroAsEmpty)\n  if (n == null) return '—'\n  return formatPercent(n)\n}\n\n/** 1-based rank_ci95 → \"rank 2\" or \"ranks 1–3\". */\nexport function formatRankCi(ci: unknown): string | null {\n  if (!Array.isArray(ci) || ci.length < 2) return null\n  const lo = finiteNumber(ci[0])\n  const hi = finiteNumber(ci[1])\n  if (lo == null || hi == null) return null\n  const a = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.min(lo, hi)))\n  const b = Math.max(a, Math.round(Math.max(lo, hi)))\n  if (a === b) return `rank ${a}`\n  return `ranks ${a}–${b}`\n}\n\nexport function formatWeightCi(ci: unknown): string | null {\n  if (!Array.isArray(ci) || ci.length < 2) return null\n  const lo = finiteNumber(ci[0])\n  const hi = finiteNumber(ci[1])\n  if (lo == null || hi == null) return null\n  return `${formatPercent(lo)}–${formatPercent(hi)}`\n}\n\nexport function formatExpectedRank(value: unknown): string | null {\n  const n = finiteNumber(value)\n  if (n == null) return null\n  const rounded = Math.round(n * 10) / 10\n  return Number.isInteger(rounded) ? String(rounded) : rounded.toFixed(1)\n}\n\n/** p_best in find_best; p_top_n in top-N modes; omit for rank_all. */\nexport function optionChanceCaption(\n  row: { p_best?: unknown, p_top_n?: unknown } | null | undefined,\n  mode: RankingMode,\n  topN?: number | null,\n): string | null {\n  if (!row) return null\n  if (mode === 'find_best') {\n    const p = nullableUnit(row.p_best)\n    if (p == null) return null\n    return `${formatPercent(p)} chance it's #1`\n  }\n  if (mode === 'find_top_3' || mode === 'find_top_half') {\n    const p = nullableUnit(row.p_top_n)\n    if (p == null) return null\n    const n = Math.max(1, finiteNumber(topN) || (mode === 'find_top_3' ? 3 : 1))\n    return `${formatPercent(p)} chance in top ${n}`\n  }\n  return null\n}\n\nexport function polarizingCaption(row: {\n  polarizing?: unknown\n  participant_rank_sd?: unknown\n  title?: string\n  expected_rank?: unknown\n  rank?: unknown\n} | null | undefined): string | null {\n  if (!row) return null\n  const sd = finiteNumber(row.participant_rank_sd)\n  const flagged = row.polarizing === true || (sd != null && sd > 1.2)\n  if (!flagged) return null\n  const er = finiteNumber(row.expected_rank) ?? (finiteNumber(row.rank) != null ? Number(row.rank) + 1 : null)\n  const rankLabel = er != null ? String(Math.round(er)) : '?'\n  const title = String(row.title || 'This option')\n  return `${title}: consensus #${rankLabel}, highly polarizing`\n}\n\nexport interface InsightChip {\n  key: 'agreement' | 'repeatability' | 'stability'\n  label: string\n  display: string\n  caption: string\n  empty: boolean\n}\n\n/**\n * Primary Results chips. Agreement = expected pair agreement.\n * Repeatability / Stability use posterior same-winner vs same-top-N;\n * rank_all does not use same-top-N (it is always 1 when top_n = n).\n */\nexport function insightMetricChips(report: Record<string, any> | null | undefined): InsightChip[] {\n  const mode = normalizeRankingMode(report?.ranking_mode, report?.exclusive_mode)\n  const coherence = report?.coherence && typeof report.coherence === 'object' ? report.coherence : {}\n  const stab = report?.posterior_stability && typeof report.posterior_stability === 'object'\n    ? report.posterior_stability\n    : {}\n  const comparisons = finiteNumber(report?.comparison_count ?? report?.total_observations) ?? 0\n  const hasData = comparisons > 0\n  const topN = Math.max(1, finiteNumber(report?.top_n) || 1)\n  const nOptions = Array.isArray(report?.option_ranking) ? report.option_ranking.length : 0\n  const topNInformative = nOptions > 1 && topN < nOptions\n\n  const agreement = nullableUnit(coherence.expected_pair_agreement, true)\n  const sameWinner = nullableUnit(stab.same_winner_repeat_probability, !hasData)\n  const sameTopN = nullableUnit(stab.same_top_n_repeat_probability, !hasData)\n\n  const useTopN = (mode === 'find_top_3' || mode === 'find_top_half') && topNInformative\n  const repeatability = useTopN ? sameTopN : sameWinner\n  const stability = useTopN ? sameWinner : (topNInformative ? sameTopN : sameWinner)\n\n  const repeatCaption = useTopN\n    ? `Same top ${topN} if these participants compared again`\n    : 'Same winner if these participants compared again'\n  const stabilityCaption = useTopN\n    ? 'Same winner with a similar new group'\n    : 'Same result with a similar new group'\n\n  return [\n    {\n      key: 'agreement',\n      label: 'Agreement',\n      display: agreement == null ? '—' : formatPercent(agreement),\n      caption: agreement == null\n        ? 'Not enough comparisons'\n        : 'How often participants would pick the same pair the same way',\n      empty: agreement == null,\n    },\n    {\n      key: 'repeatability',\n      label: 'Repeatability',\n      display: repeatability == null ? '—' : formatPercent(repeatability),\n      caption: repeatability == null ? 'Not enough comparisons' : repeatCaption,\n      empty: repeatability == null,\n    },\n    {\n      key: 'stability',\n      label: 'Stability',\n      display: stability == null ? '—' : formatPercent(stability),\n      caption: stability == null ? 'Not enough comparisons' : stabilityCaption,\n      empty: stability == null,\n    },\n  ]\n}\n\n/** Map server ranking rows into a stable UI shape (score always 0–1). */\nexport function mapServerRanking(rows: any[] | undefined | null) {\n  if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) return null\n  const n = rows.length\n  return rows.map((r, i) => {\n    // null mean_rank = option has no rank data in this slice (never compared under\n    // the factor); keep it null so the UI shows an explicit \"no data\" row.\n    const meanRank = r.mean_rank != null ? Number(r.mean_rank) : null\n    let score = r.score != null ? Number(r.score) : NaN\n    // Legacy rank-distance scores were in [0, n−1]\n    if (Number.isFinite(score) && score > 1 && n > 1) {\n      score = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, score / (n - 1)))\n    }\n    if (!Number.isFinite(score)) {\n      score = meanRank != null && n > 1 ? Math.max(0, (n - 1 - meanRank) / (n - 1)) : 0\n    }\n    const weight = r.normalizedWeight ?? r.weight ?? r.normalized_weight\n    const rankCi = Array.isArray(r.rank_ci95) ? r.rank_ci95.map(Number) : undefined\n    const weightCi = Array.isArray(r.weight_ci95) ? r.weight_ci95.map(Number) : undefined\n    return {\n      id: r.id,\n      title: r.title,\n      description: r.description,\n      score,\n      mean_rank: meanRank,\n      rank: r.rank ?? i,\n      normalizedWeight: weight != null ? Number(weight) : undefined,\n      weight: weight != null ? Number(weight) : undefined,\n      confidence: r.confidence,\n      stability: r.stability,\n      pass_consistency: r.pass_consistency,\n      leader: r.leader,\n      data_points: r.data_points ?? r.evidence ?? 0,\n      evidence: r.evidence ?? r.data_points ?? 0,\n      expected_rank: finiteNumber(r.expected_rank),\n      median_rank: finiteNumber(r.median_rank),\n      p_best: finiteNumber(r.p_best),\n      p_top_n: finiteNumber(r.p_top_n),\n      p_exact_rank: finiteNumber(r.p_exact_rank),\n      rank_ci95: rankCi?.length === 2 && rankCi.every(Number.isFinite) ? rankCi : undefined,\n      rank_entropy: finiteNumber(r.rank_entropy),\n      pairwise_win_strength: finiteNumber(r.pairwise_win_strength),\n      participant_rank_sd: finiteNumber(r.participant_rank_sd),\n      polarizing: r.polarizing === true,\n      weight_ci95: weightCi?.length === 2 && weightCi.every(Number.isFinite) ? weightCi : undefined,\n      discrimination: finiteNumber(r.discrimination),\n      leverage: finiteNumber(r.leverage),\n      p_most_important: finiteNumber(r.p_most_important),\n    }\n  })\n}\n\nexport interface PersonalSubmitterRankings {\n  options: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof mapServerRanking>>\n  factors: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof mapServerRanking>>\n  rankingMode: RankingMode\n  topN: number\n  confidence: number | null\n  showFactors: boolean\n}\n\nexport function personalRankingsFromSummary(\n  summary: Record<string, any> | null | undefined,\n  projectSettings?: Record<string, unknown> | null,\n): PersonalSubmitterRankings {\n  const options = mapServerRanking(summary?.alternative_leaderboard) || []\n  const factors = mapServerRanking(summary?.factor_leaderboard) || []\n  const rankingMode = normalizeRankingMode(\n    (projectSettings?.ranking_mode ?? summary?.ranking_mode) as string | undefined,\n    (projectSettings?.project_exclusive_mode ?? summary?.exclusive_mode) as boolean | undefined,\n  )\n  const fromSummary = finiteNumber(summary?.top_n)\n  const topN = Math.max(\n    1,\n    fromSummary ?? (rankingMode === 'find_top_3' ? 3 : 1),\n  )\n  return {\n    options,\n    factors,\n    rankingMode,\n    topN,\n    confidence: finiteNumber(summary?.confidence),\n    showFactors: factors.length > 1,\n  }\n}\n\n/** JSON often stringifies numeric map keys; tolerate number | string | null. */\nexport function lookupRankMap<T>(\n  maps: Record<string | number, T> | null | undefined,\n  key: number | string | null | undefined,\n): T | undefined {\n  if (!maps || key === undefined) return undefined\n  const rec = maps as Record<string | number, T>\n  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, key as PropertyKey)) return rec[key as keyof typeof rec]\n  if (key === null) {\n    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, 'null')) return rec.null as T\n    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, 0)) return rec[0]\n    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, '0')) return rec['0']\n    return undefined\n  }\n  const s = String(key)\n  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, s)) return rec[s]\n  const n = Number(key)\n  if (Number.isFinite(n) && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, n)) return rec[n]\n  return undefined\n}\n\n/**\n * Build a sorted ranking from a mean-rank map (lower mean_rank = better).\n * Used for per-participant per-factor #1s when only option_ranks_by_factor is present.\n */\nexport function rankingFromMeanRankMap(\n  ranksMap: Record<string | number, number> | null | undefined,\n  items: RankItem[],\n): Array<{ id: number, title: string, mean_rank: number, rank: number, score: number }> {\n  if (!ranksMap || !items?.length) return []\n  const rows = items\n    .map((item) => {\n      const raw = lookupRankMap(ranksMap, item.id)\n      if (raw == null) return null\n      const meanRank = Number(raw)\n      if (!Number.isFinite(meanRank)) return null\n      return { id: item.id, title: item.title, mean_rank: meanRank }\n    })\n    .filter((r): r is { id: number, title: string, mean_rank: number } => r != null)\n  rows.sort((a, b) => a.mean_rank - b.mean_rank || a.id - b.id)\n  const n = rows.length\n  return rows.map((r, i) => ({\n    ...r,\n    rank: i,\n    score: n > 1 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (n - 1 - r.mean_rank) / (n - 1))) : 1,\n  }))\n}\n\n/**\n * All options tied for best in a ranking (shared best mean_rank, else shared best score).\n * Ranking lists break remaining ties by id for display order — concentration must not.\n */\nexport function tiedLeadersFromRanking(\n  ranking: Array<{ id?: number, mean_rank?: number, score?: number, title?: string }> | null | undefined,\n): Array<{ id: number, title: string }> {\n  if (!ranking?.length) return []\n  const rows = ranking\n    .map(r => ({\n      id: Number(r.id),\n      title: String(r.title || ''),\n      mean_rank: r.mean_rank != null ? Number(r.mean_rank) : NaN,\n      score: r.score != null ? Number(r.score) : NaN,\n    }))\n    .filter(r => Number.isFinite(r.id))\n  if (!rows.length) return []\n\n  const withMean = rows.filter(r => Number.isFinite(r.mean_rank))\n  if (withMean.length) {\n    const best = Math.min(...withMean.map(r => r.mean_rank))\n    return withMean\n      .filter(r => Math.abs(r.mean_rank - best) < 1e-9)\n      .map(r => ({ id: r.id, title: r.title }))\n  }\n\n  const withScore = rows.filter(r => Number.isFinite(r.score))\n  if (!withScore.length) return [{ id: rows[0].id, title: rows[0].title }]\n  const bestScore = Math.max(...withScore.map(r => r.score))\n  return withScore\n    .filter(r => Math.abs(r.score - bestScore) < 1e-9)\n    .map(r => ({ id: r.id, title: r.title }))\n}\n\n/** #1 option(s) under a factor for one participant row from the report. */\nexport function participantLeadersUnderFactor(\n  participant: any,\n  factorId: number | null | undefined,\n  items: RankItem[],\n): Array<{ id: number, title: string }> {\n  if (!participant) return []\n  if (factorId == null) {\n    const ranking = mapServerRanking(\n      participant.option_ranking || participant.ranking,\n    ) || []\n    if (ranking.length) return tiedLeadersFromRanking(ranking)\n    const leader = participant.leader\n    if (leader && typeof leader === 'object' && leader.id != null) {\n      return [{ id: Number(leader.id), title: String(leader.title || '') }]\n    }\n    return []\n  }\n  const ranks = lookupRankMap(participant.option_ranks_by_factor, factorId)\n  const ranking = rankingFromMeanRankMap(ranks as Record<string | number, number> | undefined, items)\n  return tiedLeadersFromRanking(ranking)\n}\n\n/** First #1 under a factor (display convenience). */\nexport function participantLeaderUnderFactor(\n  participant: any,\n  factorId: number | null | undefined,\n  items: RankItem[],\n): { id: number, title: string } | null {\n  return participantLeadersUnderFactor(participant, factorId, items)[0] || null\n}\n\nexport function mergeRankingSettings(\n  ...sources: Array<Partial<RankingSettings> | Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined>\n): RankingSettings {\n  const base = defaultRankingSettings()\n  for (const src of sources) {\n    if (!src) continue\n    const s = src as Record<string, unknown>\n    if (s.coherenceMethod != null || s.coherence_method != null) {\n      const m = String(s.coherenceMethod ?? s.coherence_method).toLowerCase()\n      base.coherenceMethod = m === 'kendall' ? 'kendall' : 'spearman'\n    }\n  }\n  return base\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":529,"totalLines":529,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/utils/ranking.ts","time":{"start":1787325225688,"end":1787325225720}},"id":"prt_024e29ac7001PFmK4Np44zbpCJ","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_024e27d51001S07aE8jiUh6SZc"}}

multi_pass = sum(multi_passes) / len(multi_passes) if multi_passes else 0.0\n    if stab_basis == \"none\" and multi_pass > 0:\n        stability = multi_pass\n        stab_basis = \"multi_pass\"\n\n    agreement = cross_participant_agreement(bundles)\n    # With one participant, agreement is trivially 1 — down-weight in confidence via n_parts\n    n_parts = len(pids) if pids else 0\n    if n_parts < 2:\n        agreement_for_conf = multi_pass  # no peer signal; lean on repeatability\n    else:\n        agreement_for_conf = agreement\n\n    # Factor divergence from pooled mean ranks per factor (group landscape)\n    pooled_by_factor: dict[int | None, list[dict[int, float]]] = defaultdict(list)\n    for b in bundles:\n        for fid, rm in (b.get(\"option_ranks_by_factor\") or {}).items():\n            if rm:\n                pooled_by_factor[fid].append(rm)\n    merged_by_factor = {k: mean_rank_maps(v) for k, v in pooled_by_factor.items()}\n    f_div = factor_option_divergence(merged_by_factor)\n\n    min_p = clamp_min_expected_passes(cfg.get(\"min_expected_passes\"))\n    pass_fracs = []\n    for b in bundles:\n        passes = int(b.get(\"passes\") or 0)\n        pass_fracs.append(min(1.0, passes / float(min_p)))\n    if not pass_fracs and parts:\n        pass_fracs = [0.0]\n    completion = sum(pass_fracs) / len(pass_fracs) if pass_fracs else 0.0\n\n    n_facs = len([f for f in (factors or []) if not _as_dict(f).get(\"disabled\")])\n    expected_per = max(1, (n_facs if n_facs else 1) + (1 if n_facs > 1 else 0))\n    expected_total = expected_per * max(1, n_parts or 1) * max(1, min_p)\n    depth = min(1.0, len(gs) / float(expected_total)) if expected_total else 0.0\n\n    # Confidence: multi-pass + peer agreement + depth (no cross-factor penalty/reward)\n    if exclusive_mode:\n        confidence = 0.30 * multi_pass + 0.50 * agreement_for_conf + 0.20 * depth\n    else:\n        confidence = 0.35 * multi_pass + 0.45 * agreement_for_conf + 0.20 * depth\n\n    n_groups = len(gs)\n    n_comps = sum(int(g.get(\"comparison_count\") or 0) for g in gs)\n    return {\n        \"stability\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, stability))),\n        \"stability_basis\": stab_basis,\n        \"stability_channels\": stab_detail.get(\"channels\") or {},\n        \"confidence\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, confidence))),\n        \"agreement\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, agreement if n_parts >= 2 else multi_pass))),\n        \"multi_pass\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, multi_pass))),\n        \"inter_group\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, multi_pass))),  # alias\n        \"inter_participant\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, agreement if n_parts >= 2 else 0.0))),\n        \"factor_divergence\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, f_div))),\n        \"depth\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, depth))),\n        \"completion\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, completion))),\n        \"coverage\": float(max(0.0, min(1.0, completion))),\n        \"unique_participants\": n_parts if n_parts else len(parts),\n        \"group_count\": n_groups,\n        \"comparison_count\": n_comps,\n        \"observation_count\": n_comps,\n    }\n\n\ndef build_personal_submitter_summary(\n    *,\n    alternatives: list | None,\n    factors: list | None,\n    groups: list | None = None,\n    observations: list | None = None,\n    exclusive_mode: bool = False,\n    settings: dict | None = None,\n) -> dict:\n    _ = observations\n    report = build_report(\n        alternatives=alternatives,\n        factors=factors,\n        exclusive_mode=exclusive_mode,\n        all_groups=groups,\n        settings=settings,\n    )\n    ranking = report.get(\"option_ranking\") or []\n    return {\n        \"alternative_leaderboard\": ranking,\n        \"factor_leaderboard\": report.get(\"factor_ranking\") or [],\n        \"group_count\": report.get(\"group_count\") or 0,\n        \"comparison_count\": report.get(\"comparison_count\") or 0,\n        \"exclusive_mode\": exclusive_mode,\n        \"ranking_mode\": report.get(\"ranking_mode\"),\n        \"top_n\": report.get(\"top_n\"),\n        \"confidence\": report.get(\"confidence\"),\n        \"stability\": report.get(\"stability\"),\n        \"multi_pass\": report.get(\"multi_pass\"),\n    }\n\n\ndef _merge_weighted(pairs: list[tuple[dict[int, float], float]]) -> dict[int, float]:\n    if not pairs:\n        return {}\n    acc: dict[int, float] = defaultdict(float)\n    wsum: dict[int, float] = defaultdict(float)\n    for ranks, w in pairs:\n        if w <= 0:\n            continue\n        for k, r in ranks.items():\n            acc[k] += w * r\n            wsum[k] += w\n    return {k: acc[k] / wsum[k] for k in acc if wsum[k] > 0}\n\n\ndef build_report(\n    *,\n    alternatives: list | None,\n    factors: list | None,\n    exclusive_mode: bool = False,\n    all_groups: list | None = import { test } from 'node:test'\n2: import assert from 'node:assert/strict'\n3: import {\n4:   formatRankCi,\n5:   formatNullablePercent,\n6:   formatExpectedRank,\n7:   formatWeightCi,\n8:   headlineOptionLabel,\n9:   normalizeRankingMode,\n10:   optionChanceCaption,\n11:   polarizingCaption,\n12:   insightMetricChips,\n13:   mapServerRanking,\n14:   personalRankingsFromSummary,\n15:   metricLabel,\n16: } from './ranking.ts'\n17: \n18: test('formatRankCi rounds 1-based bounds', () => {\n19:   assert.equal(formatRankCi([1.1, 3.4]), 'ranks 1–3')\n20:   assert.equal(formatRankCi([2, 2]), 'rank 2')\n21:   assert.equal(formatRankCi(null), null)\n22:   assert.equal(formatRankCi([1]), null)\n23: })\n24: \n25: test('optionChanceCaption follows ranking mode', () => {\n26:   const row = { p_best: 0.62, p_top_n: 0.81 }\n27:   assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'find_best', 1), \"62% chance it's #1\")\n28:   assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'find_top_3', 3), '81% chance in top 3')\n29:   assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'find_top_half', 4), '81% chance in top 4')\n30:   assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'rank_all', 6), null)\n31: })\n32: \n33: test('insight chips use expected pair agreement and do not show 0% as empty agreement', () => {\n34:   const chips = insightMetricChips({\n35:     ranking_mode: 'find_best',\n36:     exclusive_mode: true,\n37:     comparison_count: 12,\n38:     top_n: 1,\n39:     option_ranking: [{ id: 1 }, { id: 2 }],\n40:     coherence: { expected_pair_agreement: 0.74 },\n41:     posterior_stability: {\n42:       same_winner_repeat_probability: 0.55,\n43:       same_top_n_repeat_probability: 0.55,\n44:     },\n45:   })\n46:   assert.deepEqual(chips.map(c => c.label), ['Agreement', 'Repeatability', 'Stability'])\n47:   assert.equal(chips[0].display, '74%')\n48:   assert.equal(chips[1].display, '55%')\n49:   assert.match(chips[0].caption, /pair/)\n50: })\n51: \n52: test('insight chips treat missing agreement as not enough comparisons', () => {\n53:   const chips = insightMetricChips({\n54:     ranking_mode: 'rank_all',\n55:     comparison_count: 0,\n56:     coherence: { expected_pair_agreement: 0 },\n57:     posterior_stability: {},\n58:   })\n59:   assert.equal(chips[0].display, '—')\n60:   assert.equal(chips[0].caption, 'Not enough comparisons')\n61:   assert.equal(chips[1].display, '—')\n62: })\n63: \n64: test('top-N mode uses same_top_n for Repeatability', () => {\n65:   const chips = insightMetricChips({\n66:     ranking_mode: 'find_top_3',\n67:     comparison_count: 20,\n68:     top_n: 3,\n69:     option_ranking: [{ id: 1 }, { id: 2 }, { id: 3 }, { id: 4 }],\n70:     coherence: { expected_pair_agreement: 0.6 },\n71:     posterior_stability: {\n72:       same_winner_repeat_probability: 0.4,\n73:       same_top_n_repeat_probability: 0.7,\n74:     },\n75:   })\n76:   assert.equal(chips[1].display, '70%')\n77:   assert.equal(chips[2].display, '40%')\n78: })\n79: \n80: test('headline and polarizing copy', () => {\n81:   assert.equal(headlineOptionLabel('find_best'), 'Recommended option')\n82: 2')","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/utils/ranking.test.ts","text":"import { test } from 'node:test'\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict'\nimport {\n  formatRankCi,\n  formatNullablePercent,\n  formatExpectedRank,\n  formatWeightCi,\n  headlineOptionLabel,\n  normalizeRankingMode,\n  optionChanceCaption,\n  polarizingCaption,\n  insightMetricChips,\n  mapServerRanking,\n  personalRankingsFromSummary,\n  metricLabel,\n} from './ranking.ts'\n\ntest('formatRankCi rounds 1-based bounds', () => {\n  assert.equal(formatRankCi([1.1, 3.4]), 'ranks 1–3')\n  assert.equal(formatRankCi([2, 2]), 'rank 2')\n  assert.equal(formatRankCi(null), null)\n  assert.equal(formatRankCi([1]), null)\n})\n\ntest('optionChanceCaption follows ranking mode', () => {\n  const row = { p_best: 0.62, p_top_n: 0.81 }\n  assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'find_best', 1), \"62% chance it's #1\")\n  assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'find_top_3', 3), '81% chance in top 3')\n  assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'find_top_half', 4), '81% chance in top 4')\n  assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(row, 'rank_all', 6), null)\n})\n\ntest('insight chips use expected pair agreement and do not show 0% as empty agreement', () => {\n  const chips = insightMetricChips({\n    ranking_mode: 'find_best',\n    exclusive_mode: true,\n    comparison_count: 12,\n    top_n: 1,\n    option_ranking: [{ id: 1 }, { id: 2 }],\n    coherence: { expected_pair_agreement: 0.74 },\n    posterior_stability: {\n      same_winner_repeat_probability: 0.55,\n      same_top_n_repeat_probability: 0.55,\n    },\n  })\n  assert.deepEqual(chips.map(c => c.label), ['Agreement', 'Repeatability', 'Stability'])\n  assert.equal(chips[0].display, '74%')\n  assert.equal(chips[1].display, '55%')\n  assert.match(chips[0].caption, /pair/)\n})\n\ntest('insight chips treat missing agreement as not enough comparisons', () => {\n  const chips = insightMetricChips({\n    ranking_mode: 'rank_all',\n    comparison_count: 0,\n    coherence: { expected_pair_agreement: 0 },\n    posterior_stability: {},\n  })\n  assert.equal(chips[0].display, '—')\n  assert.equal(chips[0].caption, 'Not enough comparisons')\n  assert.equal(chips[1].display, '—')\n})\n\ntest('top-N mode uses same_top_n for Repeatability', () => {\n  const chips = insightMetricChips({\n    ranking_mode: 'find_top_3',\n    comparison_count: 20,\n    top_n: 3,\n    option_ranking: [{ id: 1 }, { id: 2 }, { id: 3 }, { id: 4 }],\n    coherence: { expected_pair_agreement: 0.6 },\n    posterior_stability: {\n      same_winner_repeat_probability: 0.4,\n      same_top_n_repeat_probability: 0.7,\n    },\n  })\n  assert.equal(chips[1].display, '70%')\n  assert.equal(chips[2].display, '40%')\n})\n\ntest('headline and polarizing copy', () => {\n  assert.equal(headlineOptionLabel('find_best'), 'Recommended option')\n  assert.equal(headlineOptionLabel('rank_all'), 'Top option')\n  assert.equal(\n    polarizingCaption({ title: 'Option A', expected_rank: 2.2, polarizing: true }),\n    'Option A: consensus #2, highly polarizing',\n  )\n  assert.equal(polarizingCaption({ title: 'Calm', polarizing: false }), null)\n})\n\ntest('mapServerRanking forwards posterior fields', () => {\n  const mapped = mapServerRanking([\n    {\n      id: 9,\n      title: 'Alpha',\n      score: 0.71,\n      mean_rank: 0.4,\n      p_best: 0.66,\n      p_top_n: 0.9,\n      rank_ci95: [1, 2],\n      expected_rank: 1.4,\n      polarizing: true,\n      discrimination: 0.12,\n      leverage: 0.4,\n      weight_ci95: [0.2, 0.35],\n      normalizedWeight: 0.28,\n    },\n  ])\n  assert.ok(mapped)\n  assert.equal(mapped[0].p_best, 0.66)\n  assert.deepEqual(mapped[0].rank_ci95, [1, 2])\n  assert.equal(mapped[0].polarizing, true)\n  assert.equal(mapped[0].leverage, 0.4)\n  assert.equal(formatRankCi(mapped[0].rank_ci95), 'ranks 1–2')\n  assert.equal(formatExpectedRank(mapped[0].expected_rank), '1.4')\n  assert.equal(formatWeightCi(mapped[0].weight_ci95), '20%–35%')\n  assert.equal(formatNullablePercent(null), '—')\n  assert.equal(normalizeRankingMode('pick_one'), 'find_best')\n})\n\ntest('personalRankingsFromSummary maps vote-view thank-you payload', () => {\n  const mapped = personalRankingsFromSummary(\n    {\n      ranking_mode: 'rank_all',\n      top_n: 2,\n      confidence: 0.72,\n      exclusive_mode: false,\n      alternative_leaderboard: [\n        { id: 1, title: 'Alpha', score: 0.8, rank_ci95: [1, 2], expected_rank: 1.2, p_best: 0.7 },\n        { id: 2, title: 'Beta', score: 0.2, rank_ci95: [1, 2], expected_rank: 1.8 },\n      ],\n      factor_leaderboard: [\n        { id: 10, title: 'Cost', weight: 0.62, weight_ci95: [0.4, 0.8], discrimination: 0.3, leverage: 0.5 },\n        { id: 11, title: 'Quality', weight: 0.38, discrimination: 0.2, leverage: 0.4 },\n      ],\n    },\n    { ranking_mode: 'find_best' },\n  )\n  assert.equal(mapped.options.length, 2)\n  assert.equal(mapped.factors.length, 2)\n  assert.equal(mapped.showFactors, true)\n  assert.equal(mapped.rankingMode, 'find_best')\n  assert.equal(mapped.topN, 2)\n  assert.equal(mapped.confidence, 0.72)\n  assert.equal(metricLabel(mapped.confidence ?? 0), 'Solid')\n  assert.equal(formatRankCi(mapped.options[0].rank_ci95), 'ranks 1–2')\n  assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(mapped.options[0], mapped.rankingMode, mapped.topN), \"70% chance it's #1\")\n  assert.equal(mapped.factors[0].weight, 0.62)\n})\n\ntest('personalRankingsFromSummary hides factor pane for a single factor', () => {\n  const mapped = personalRankingsFromSummary({\n    ranking_mode: 'rank_all',\n    alternative_leaderboard: [{ id: 1, title: 'Only', score: 1 }],\n    factor_leaderboard: [{ id: 10, title: 'Overall', weight: 1 }],\n  })\n  assert.equal(mapped.showFactors, false)\n  assert.equal(mapped.rankingMode, 'rank_all')\n  assert.equal(optionChanceCaption(mapped.options[0], mapped.rankingMode, mapped.topN), null)\n})","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":159,"totalLines":159,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/utils/ranking.test.ts","time":{"start":1787325258713,"end":1787325258792}},"id":"prt_024e31ba40019LtkGsSAhmB6KO","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_024e2fb5c001zlRFIaHFReOdXG"}}

legacy groups.\"\"\"\nfrom src.utils.bt_inference.cache import clear as clear_bt_cache\nfrom src.utils.bt_inference import laplace as bt_laplace\nfrom src.utils.bt_inference.laplace import reset_fit_calls\nfrom src.utils.bt_inference.observations import extract_observations\nfrom src.utils.bt_inference.ranks import posterior_ranks, rank_summary\nfrom src.utils.vote_ranking import build_report\n\n\ndef setup_function(_fn=None):\n    clear_bt_cache()\n    reset_fit_calls()\n\n\ndef _alts(n=4):\n    return [{\"id\": i, \"alternative_title\": chr(64 + i)} for i in range(1, n + 1)]\n\n\ndef _facs():\n    return [\n        {\"id\": 10, \"factor_title\": \"Cost\"},\n        {\"id\": 11, \"factor_title\": \"Quality\"},\n    ]\n\n\ndef _adjacent(order):\n    return [{\"winner_id\": a, \"loser_id\": b, \"response\": \"winner\"} for a, b in zip(order, order[1:])]\n\n\ndef _group(pid, order, *, fid=None, gtype=\"alternative\", extra_pairs=None, status=\"complete\", gid=None):\n    pairs = list(extra_pairs) if extra_pairs is not None else _adjacent(order)\n    return {\n        \"id\": gid,\n        \"participant_id\": pid,\n        \"group_type\": gtype,\n        \"criterion_id\": fid,\n        \"pass_index\": 1,\n        \"rank_order\": order,\n        \"pairings\": pairs,\n        \"comparison_count\": len(pairs),\n        \"status\": status,\n    }\n\n\ndef test_posterior_ranks_and_summary_strong_order():\n    import numpy as np\n\n    scores = np.array([[4.0, 2.0, 0.0]] * 50)\n    ranks = posterior_ranks(scores)\n    assert ranks.shape == (50, 3)\n    assert (ranks[:, 0] == 1).all()\n    summary = rank_summary(scores, top_n=1)\n    by_item = {s[\"item\"]: s for s in summary}\n    assert by_item[0][\"rank\"] == 1\n    assert by_item[0][\"p_best\"] == 1.0\n    assert by_item[2][\"p_best\"] == 0.0\n\n\ndef test_strong_winner_high_p_best():\n    order = [1, 2, 3, 4]\n    pairs = [\n        {\"winner_id\": 1, \"loser_id\": j, \"response\": \"winner\"}\n        for j in (2, 3, 4)\n    ] + [\n        {\"winner_id\": 2, \"loser_id\": 3, \"response\": \"winner\"},\n        {\"winner_id\": 2, \"loser_id\": 4, \"response\": \"winner\"},\n        {\"winner_id\": 3, \"loser_id\": 4, \"response\": \"winner\"},\n    ]\n    groups = [_group(1, order, fid=None, extra_pairs=pairs)]\n    report = build_report(\n        alternatives=_alts(4),\n        factors=[],\n        all_groups=groups,\n        participants=[{\"id\": 1}],\n        settings={\"bt_n_samples\": 400, \"ranking_mode\": \"find_best\"},\n    )\n    ranking = report[\"option_ranking\"]\n    assert ranking[0][\"id\"] == 1\n    assert float(ranking[0][\"p_best\"]) > 0.7\n    assert 0.0 <= float(ranking[0][\"score\"]) <= assert max(float(r[\"p_best\"]) for r in top) < 0.9\n\n\ndef test_clear_top3_vs_uncertain_boundary():\n    # 1,2,3 clearly above 4,5; 3 vs 4 is the close call\n    pairs = []\n    for w, l in ((1, 4), assert float(by_id[5][\"p_top_n\"]) < 0.4\n    assert report[\"top_n\"] == 3\n\n\ndef test_polarized_participants_not_labeled_unreliable():\n    g1 = _group(1, [1, 2, 3, 4], fid=None, gid=1)\n    g2 = _group(2, [4, 3, 2, 1], fid=None, gid=2)\n    report = build_report(\n        alternatives=_alts(4),\n        factors=[],\n        all_groups=[g1, g2],\n        participants=[{\"id\": 1}, {\"id\": p_bests = [float(r[\"p_best\"]) for r in report[\"option_ranking\"]]\n    assert max(p_bests) < 0.85\n    assert report[\"coherence\"][\"directional\"] < 0.85\n\n\ndef test_important_but_flat_factor_modest_leverage():\n    groups = [\n        _group(1, [1, 2, 3], fid=10, gid=1),\n        _group(\n            1,\n            [1, 2, 3],\n            fid=11,\n            gid=2,\n            extra_pairs=[\n                {\"winner_id\": 1, \"loser_id\": 2, \"response\": \"tie\"},\n                {\"winner_id\": 2, \"loser_id\": 3, \"response\": \"tie\"},\n                {\"winner_id\": 1, \"loser_id\": 3, \"response\": \"tie\"},\n            ],\n        ),\n        _group(1, [10, 11], gtype=\"criteria\", gid=3),\n    ]\n    report = build_report(\n        alternatives=_alts(3),\n        factors=_facs(),\n        all_groups=groups,\n        participants=[{\"id\": 1}],\n        settings={\"bt_n_samples\": 300},\n    )\n    by_id = {int(r[\"id\"]): r for by_id[11].get(\"weight\") or 0\n    )\n    assert float(by_id[11].get(\"discrimination\") or 0) <= float(by_id[10].get(\"discrimination\") or 1)\n    if by_id[11].get(\"leverage\") is not None:\n {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787325778285,"sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"edit","callID":"call_5a49d50c782a4d65b8893822","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py","newString":"def test_posterior_ranks_and_summary_strong_order():\n    import numpy as np\n\n    scores = np.array([[4.0, 2.0, 0.0]] * 50)\n    ranks = posterior_ranks(scores)\n    assert ranks.shape == (50, 3)\n    assert (ranks[:, 0] == 1).all()\n    summary = rank_summary(scores, top_n=1)\n    by_item = {s[\"item\"]: s for s in summary}\n    assert by_item[0][\"rank\"] == 1\n    assert by_item[0][\"p_best\"] == 1.0\n    assert by_item[2][\"p_best\"] == 0.0\n\n\ndef test_rank_summary_exposes_rank_sd():\n    import numpy as np\n\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)\n    scores = np.column_stack([\n        np.full(400, 6.0),\n        2.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 400),\n        -2.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 400),\n    ])\n    summary = rank_summary(scores, top_n=1)\n    by_item = {s[\"item\"]: s for s in summary}\n    for row in summary:\n        assert row[\"rank_sd\"] >= 0.0\n    # dominant item always ranks #1 → near-zero spread; coin-flip pair spreads wide\n    assert by_item[0][\"rank_sd\"] < 0.05\n    assert by_item[0][\"rank_sd\"] < by_item[1][\"rank_sd\"]","oldString":"def test_posterior_ranks_and_summary_strong_order():\n    import numpy as np\n\n    scores = np.array([[4.0, 2.0, 0.0]] * 50)\n    ranks = posterior_ranks(scores)\n    assert ranks.shape == (50, 3)\n    assert (ranks[:, 0] == 1).all()\n    summary = rank_summary(scores, top_n=1)\n    by_item = {s[\"item\"]: s for s in summary}\n    assert by_item[0][\"rank\"] == 1\n    assert by_item[0][\"p_best\"] == 1.0\n    assert by_item[2][\"p_best\"] == 0.0"},"output":"Edit applied successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"diff":"Index: /workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py\n===================================================================\n--- /workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py\n+++ /workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py\n@@ -55,8 +55,26 @@\n     assert by_item[0][\"p_best\"] == 1.0\n     assert by_item[2][\"p_best\"] == 0.0\n \n \n+def test_rank_summary_exposes_rank_sd():\n+    import numpy as np\n+\n+    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)\n+    scores = np.column_stack([\n+        np.full(400, 6.0),\n+        2.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 400),\n+        -2.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 400),\n+    ])\n+    summary = rank_summary(scores, top_n=1)\n+    by_item = {s[\"item\"]: s for s in summary}\n+    for row in summary:\n+        assert row[\"rank_sd\"] >= 0.0\n+    # dominant item always ranks #1 → near-zero spread; coin-flip pair spreads wide\n+    assert by_item[0][\"rank_sd\"] < 0.05\n+    assert by_item[0][\"rank_sd\"] < by_item[1][\"rank_sd\"]\n+\n+\n def test_strong_winner_high_p_best():\n     order = [1, 2, 3, 4]\n     pairs = [\n         {\"winner_id\": 1, \"loser_id\": j, \"response\": \"winner\"}\n","filediff":{"file":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py","patch":"Index: /workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py\n===================================================================\n--- /workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py\n+++ /workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_bt_inference.py\n@@ -55,8 +55,26 @@\n     assert by_item[0][\"p_best\"] == 1.0\n     assert by_item[2][\"p_best\"] == 0.0\n \n \n+def test_rank_summary_exposes_rank_sd():\n+    import numpy as np\n+\n+    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)\n+    scores = np.column_stack([\n+        np.full(400, 6.0),\n+        2.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 400),\n+        -2.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, 400),\n+    ])\n+    summary = rank_summary(scores, top_n=1)\n+    by_item = {s[\"item\"]: s for s in summary}\n+    for row in summary:\n+        assert row[\"rank_sd\"] >= 0.0\n+    # dominant item always ranks #1 → near-zero spread; coin-flip pair spreads wide\n+    assert by_item[0][\"rank_sd\"] < 0.05\n+    assert by_item[0][\"rank_sd\"] < by_item[1][\"rank_sd\"]\n+\n+\n def test_strong_winner_high_p_best():\n     order = [1, 2, 3, 4]\n     pairs = [\n         {\"winner_id\": 1, \"loser_id\": j, \"response\": \"winner\"}\n","additions":18,"deletions":0},"truncated":false},"title":"src/tests/test_bt_inference.py","time":{"start":1787325778212,"end":1787325778232}},"id":"prt_024eb084e001DuTxHr7GsMy7IG","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_024ea9ff8001PIUoY9wquFuWWI"}}
