{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787326048455,"sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_8d882f4e59244bcf97c5adc8","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"content":"/**\n * Geometry for the self-view expected-rank range bar.\n *\n * Paints a compact horizontal rank axis (1…N) with a color gradient peaking at\n * the posterior expected rank and fading to the background at ±2 SD. Pure math\n * only — RankRangeBar.vue turns the model into markup.\n */\n\nexport interface RankRangeBarInput {\n  expectedRank?: number | null\n  rankSd?: number | null\n  /** 95% rank interval (1-based); used to approximate SD when rank_sd is missing. */\n  rankCi95?: number[] | null\n  /** Number of ranked items; defines the axis extent. */\n  itemCount: number\n}\n\nexport interface RankGradientStop {\n  /** Percent within the painted band (0–100). */\n  offset: number\n  /** Peak color intensity 0–1 at this stop. */\n  alpha: number\n}\n\nexport interface RankTick {\n  /** Percent across the full axis (0–100). */\n  offset: number\n  /** Numeric label; null for unlabeled minor ticks. */\n  label: string | null\n}\n\nexport interface RankRangeBarModel {\n  bandStart: number\n  bandEnd: number\n  stops: RankGradientStop[]\n  ticks: RankTick[]\n  /** Axis percent of the expected-rank peak. */\n  peakOffset: number\n}\n\n/** Gradient reaches zero intensity at mean ± this many SDs. */\nexport const RANGE_BAR_SD_SPAN = 2\n/** 7 gradient stops: mean, ±⅔ SD, ±4⁄3 SD, ±2 SD. */\nexport const RANGE_BAR_STOP_ALPHAS = [0, 0.15, 0.45, 0.88, 0.45, 0.15, 0]\n/** Smallest painted band (in rank units) so near-certain items stay visible. */\nexport const RANGE_BAR_MIN_SPAN = 0.2\n\nconst TICK_BUDGET = 9\n\nfunction finite(value: unknown): number | null {\n  const n = Number(value)\n  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null\n}\n\n/**\n * SD implied by a 95% interval of a normal distribution.\n * Returns null when the CI is missing/degenerate (zero-width intervals carry\n * no spread information).\n */\nexport function sdFromRankCi(ci: unknown): number | null {\n  if (!Array.isArray(ci) || ci.length < 2) return null\n  const lo = finite(ci[0])\n  const hi = finite(ci[1])\n  if (lo == null || hi == null) return null\n  const sd = Math.abs(hi - lo) / 3.92\n  return sd > 0 ? sd : null\n}\n\nfunction axisPercent(rank: number, itemCount: number): number {\n  if (itemCount <= 1) return 50\n  return ((rank - 1) / (itemCount - 1)) * 100\n}\n\nfunction buildTicks(itemCount: number): RankTick[] {\n  if (itemCount < 2) return []\n  const step = itemCount <= TICK_BUDGET ? 1 : Math.ceil(itemCount / TICK_BUDGET)\n  const labeledAll = itemCount <= 8\n  const ticks: RankTick[] = []\n  const seen = new Set<number>()\n  const push = (rank: number) => {\n    if (seen.has(rank)) return\n    seen.add(rank)\n    ticks.push({\n      offset: axisPercent(rank, itemCount),\n      label: labeledAll || rank === 1 || rank === itemCount ? String(rank) : null,\n    })\n  }\n  for (let r = 1; r <= itemCount; r += step) push(r)\n  push(itemCount)\n  return ticks.sort((a, b) => a.offset - b.offset)\n}\n\n/**\n * Build the paint model, or null when there is not enough data to draw\n * (no expected rank, no SD derivable, or fewer than two positions).\n */\nexport function buildRankRangeBar(input: RankRangeBarInput): RankRangeBarModel | null {\n  const n = Math.floor(finite(input.itemCount) ?? 0)\n  const mean = finite(input.expectedRank)\n  if (n < 2 || mean == null) return null\n  const sdRaw = finite(input.rankSd)\n  const sd = sdRaw != null && sdRaw >= 0 ? sdRaw : sdFromRankCi(input.rankCi95)\n  if (sd == null) return null\n\n  const clampedMean = Math.min(Math.max(mean, 1), n)\n  let bandLo = Math.max(1, clampedMean - RANGE_BAR_SD_SPAN * sd)\n  let bandHi = Math.min(n, clampedMean + RANGE_BAR_SD_SPAN * sd)\n  if (bandHi - bandLo < RANGE_BAR_MIN_SPAN) {\n    const half = RANGE_BAR_MIN_SPAN / 2\n    bandLo = Math.max(1, clampedMean - half)\n    bandHi = Math.min(n, Math.max(bandLo + RANGE_BAR_MIN_SPAN, clampedMean + half))\n  }\n  const bandSpan = bandHi - bandLo\n\n  const merged = new Map<number, number>()\n  merged.set(0, 0)\n  merged.set(100, 0)\n  const steps = [-1, -2 / 3, -1 / 3, 0, 1 / 3, 2 / 3, 1]\n  steps.forEach((step, i) => {\n    const rankPos = clampedMean + step * RANGE_BAR_SD_SPAN * sd\n    const frac = (rankPos - bandLo) / bandSpan\n    if (frac < 0 || frac > 1) return\n    const offset = Math.round(frac * 10000) / 100\n    const alpha = RANGE_BAR_STOP_ALPHAS[i]\n    merged.set(offset, Math.max(merged.get(offset) ?? 0, alpha))\n  })\n  const stops = [...merged.entries()]\n    .map(([offset, alpha]) => ({ offset, alpha }))\n    .sort((a, b) => a.offset - b.offset)\n\n  return {\n    bandStart: axisPercent(bandLo, n),\n    bandEnd: axisPercent(bandHi, n),\n    stops,\n    ticks: buildTicks(n),\n    peakOffset: axisPercent(clampedMean, n),\n  }\n}\n\n/** CSS background for the painted band from a model's stops. */\nexport function rankRangeGradient(stops: RankGradientStop[]): string {\n  const body = stops\n    .map(s => `rgba(var(--pc-primary-rgb), ${s.alpha}) ${s.offset}%`)\n    .join(', ')\n  return `linear-gradient(90deg, ${body})`\n}\n","filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/utils/rankRangeBar.ts"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/utils/rankRangeBar.ts","exists":false,"truncated":false},"title":"client/utils/rankRangeBar.ts","time":{"start":1787326048367,"end":1787326048435}},"id":"prt_024ef2828001JyXu2c9U2T3ioC","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_024ed9009001UEuobigJjAbWK9"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787326082116,"sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_15fab2838c69481d875ba548","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"content":"<template>\n  <span class=\"rank-range-bar\" :class=\"`rank-range-bar--${density}`\" :title=\"TOOLTIP_TEXT\">\n    <template v-if=\"model\">\n      <span class=\"d-sr-only\">{{ srText }}</span>\n      <span class=\"rank-range-bar__viz\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n        <span class=\"rank-range-bar__axis\" />\n        <span\n          v-for=\"(tick, i) in model.ticks\"\n          :key=\"`tick-${i}`\"\n          class=\"rank-range-bar__tick\"\n          :style=\"{ left: `${tick.offset}%` }\"\n        />\n        <span class=\"rank-range-bar__band\" :style=\"bandStyle\" />\n        <span class=\"rank-range-bar__peak\" :style=\"{ left: `${model.peakOffset}%` }\" />\n        <template v-if=\"showTickLabels\">\n          <span\n            v-for=\"(tick, i) in model.ticks\"\n            :key=\"`label-${i}`\"\n            class=\"rank-range-bar__tick-label\"\n            :style=\"{ left: `${tick.offset}%` }\"\n          >{{ tick.label }}</span>\n        </template>\n      </span>\n    </template>\n    <template v-else>\n      <slot name=\"fallback\" />\n    </template>\n  </span>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport {\n  buildRankRangeBar,\n  rankRangeGradient,\n  type RankRangeBarModel,\n} from '~/utils/rankRangeBar'\nimport { formatExpectedRank, formatRankCi } from '~/utils/ranking'\n\nexport interface RankRangeBarProps {\n  expectedRank?: number | null\n  rankSd?: number | null\n  rankCi95?: Array<number> | null\n  itemCount?: number | null\n  /** compact hides numeric labels for dense list rows; comfortable shows them. */\n  density?: 'compact' | 'comfortable'\n}\n\nconst props = withDefaults(defineProps<RankRangeBarProps>(), {\n  expectedRank: null,\n  rankSd: null,\n  rankCi95: null,\n  itemCount: 0,\n  density: 'compact',\n})\n\nconst TOOLTIP_TEXT = 'visually shows the likely rank range of this item'\n\nconst model = computed<RankRangeBarModel | null>(() =>\n  buildRankRangeBar({\n    expectedRank: props.expectedRank,\n    rankSd: props.rankSd,\n    rankCi95: props.rankCi95,\n    itemCount: Number(props.itemCount || 0),\n  }),\n)\n\nconst bandStyle = computed(() => {\n  if (!model.value) return {}\n  return {\n    left: `${model.value.bandStart}%`,\n    width: `${Math.max(0, model.value.bandEnd - model.value.bandStart)}%`,\n    background: rankRangeGradient(model.value.stops),\n  }\n})\n\nconst showTickLabels = computed(() =>\n  props.density === 'comfortable' && (model.value?.ticks.some(t => t.label != null) ?? false),\n)\n\nconst srText = computed(() => {\n  const parts: string[] = []\n  const ci = formatRankCi(props.rankCi95)\n  if (ci) parts.push(ci)\n  const expected = formatExpectedRank(props.expectedRank)\n  if (expected) parts.push(`expected ${expected}`)\n  return parts.length ? `Likely rank range: ${parts.join(', ')}` : 'Likely rank range unavailable'\n})\n</script>\n\n<style scoped>\n.rank-range-bar {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n.rank-range-bar__viz {\n  position: relative;\n  display: inline-block;\n  width: 130px;\n}\n.rank-range-bar--compact .rank-range-bar__viz {\n  height: 10px;\n}\n.rank-range-bar--comfortable .rank-range-bar__viz {\n  height: 16px;\n}\n.rank-range-bar__axis {\n  position: absolute;\n  left: 0;\n  right: 0;\n  top: 50%;\n  height: 1px;\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\n  background: rgba(var(--v-theme-on-surface), 0.18);\n}\n.rank-range-bar__tick {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 50%;\n  width: 1px;\n  height: 6px;\n  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);\n  background: rgba(var(--v-theme-on-surface), 0.3);\n}\n.rank-range-bar__band {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 0;\n  bottom: auto;\n  height: 100%;\n  border-radius: 5px;\n}\n.rank-range-bar--comfortable .rank-range-bar__band {\n  border-radius: 7px;\n}\n.rank-range-bar__peak {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 50%;\n  width: 2px;\n  height: calc(100% + 2px);\n  transform: translate(-50%, -50%);\n  background: rgb(var(--pc-primary-rgb));\n  border-radius: 1px;\n}\n.rank-range-bar__tick-label {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 100%;\n  transform: translateX(-50%);\n  margin-top: 1px;\n  font-size: 9px;\n  line-height: 1;\n  color: rgba(var(--v-theme-on-surface), 0.55);\n}\n</style>\n","filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/components/RankRangeBar.vue"},"output":"Wrote file :expected-rank=\"item.expected_rank\"\n                            :rank-sd=\"item.rank_sd\"\n                            :rank-ci95=\"item.rank_ci95\"\n {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787326183956,"sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_b17f5ef6dc1944f38bb3b86e","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"content":"import { test } from 'node:test'\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict'\nimport {\n  RANGE_BAR_SD_SPAN,\n  buildRankRangeBar,\n  rankRangeGradient,\n  sdFromRankCi,\n} from './rankRangeBar.ts'\n\ntest('sdFromRankCi divides a 95% interval and rejects degenerate CIs', () => {\n  assert.ok(Math.abs((sdFromRankCi([1, 4.92]) ?? 0) - 1) < 0.001)\n  assert.equal(sdFromRankCi([2, 2]), null)\n  assert.equal(sdFromRankCi(null), null)\n  assert.equal(sdFromRankCi([1]), null)\n})\n\ntest('buildRankRangeBar needs mean, derivable SD, and at least two positions', () => {\n  assert.equal(buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 2, rankSd: 0.5, itemCount: 5 }), null)\n  assert.equal(buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 2, rankSd: 0.5, itemCount: 1 }), null)\n  assert.equal(buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: null, rankSd: 0.5, itemCount: 5 }), null)\n  assert.equal(\n    buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 2, itemCount: 5 }),\n    null,\n    'no sd and no CI → not renderable',\n  )\n})\n\ntest('buildRankRangeBar places 7 stops fading to zero across the ±2 SD band', () => {\n  const m = buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 3, rankSd: 0.75, itemCount: 9 })\n  assert.ok(m)\n  // ±2·0.75 = 1.5 ranks around mean 3 → band [1.5, 4.5] of axis 1…9\n  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandStart - (0.5 / 8) * 100) < 0.01)\n  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.bandEnd - (3.5 / 8) * 100) < 0.01)\n  assert.equal(m.stops.length, 7)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[0].offset, 0)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[0].alpha, 0)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[6].offset, 100)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[6].alpha, 0)\n  const peak = m.stops.find(s => s.offset === 50)\n  assert.ok(peak && peak.alpha > 0.8)\n  // monotone rise then fall\n  const alphas = m.stops.map(s => s.alpha)\n  for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) assert.ok(alphas[i] >= alphas[i - 1])\n  for (let i = 4; i < alphas.length - 1; i++) assert.ok(alphas[i] >= alphas[i + 1])\n  assert.ok(Math.abs(m.peakOffset - ((3 - 1) / 8) * 100) < 0.01)\n})\n\ntest('buildRankRangeBar clips the band to ranks 1..N', () => {\n  const m = buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 1, rankSd: 2, itemCount: 5 })\n  assert.ok(m)\n  assert.equal(m.bandStart, 0)\n  assert.equal(m.bandEnd, 100 * Math.min(5, 1 + 2 * RANGE_BAR_SD_SPAN * 2 - 1) / 4)\n  // stops beyond the clip are dropped; edges stay transparent\n  assert.equal(m.stops[0].alpha, 0)\n  assert.ok(m.stops.every(s => s.offset >= 0 && s.offset <= 100))\n})\n\ntest('buildRankRangeBar keeps a minimal visible band for near-certain items', () => {\n  const m = buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 1, rankSd: 0.001, itemCount: 6 })\n  assert.ok(m)\n  const spanPct = m.bandEnd - m.bandStart\n  const minPct = (0.2 / 5) * 100\n  assert.ok(spanPct >= minPct - 0.01, `band ${spanPct} should be at least ${minPct}`)\n})\n\ntest('buildRankRangeBar labels every tick up to eight positions, ends only above', () => {\n  const small = buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 2, rankSd: 0.4, itemCount: 4 })\n  assert.ok(small)\n  assert.deepEqual(small.ticks.map(t => t.label), ['1', '2', '3', '4'])\n  assert.ok(Math.abs(small.ticks[3].offset - 100) < 0.001)\n\n  const big = buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 6, rankSd: 1, itemCount: 20 })\n  assert.ok(big)\n  const labels = big.ticks.filter(t => t.label != null).map(t => t.label)\n  assert.deepEqual(labels, ['1', '20'])\n  assert.ok(big.ticks.length <= 9)\n})\n\ntest('buildRankRangeBar falls back to CI-derived SD', () => {\n  // ci [1, 4.92] ≈ sd 1 → band [−1, 3] clipped to [1, 3] on a 5-item axis\n  const m = buildRankRangeBar({ expectedRank: 2, rankCi95: [1, 4.92], itemCount: 5 })\n  assert.ok(m, 'CI fallback should render')\n})\n\ntest('rankRangeGradient emits rgba stops on the primary token', () => {\n  const css = rankRangeGradient([\n    { offset: 0, alpha: 0 },\n    { offset: 50, alpha: 0.88 },\n    { offset: 100, alpha: 0 },\n  ])\n  assert.match(css, /^linear-gradient\\(90deg, /)\n  assert.match(css, /rgba\\(var\\(--pc-primary-rgb\\), 0\\.88\\) 50%/)\n})\n","filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/utils/rankRangeBar.test.ts"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/utils/rankRangeBar.test.ts","exists":false,"truncated":false},"title":"client/utils/rankRangeBar.test.ts","time":{"start":1787326183874,"end":1787326183940}},"id":"prt_024f139a1001OtLCJVx5l91q8j","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_024f0cae3001kI0OB3v3BwVOf9"}}

clip to the full [1, 5] axis\n  assert.equal(m.bandStart, 0)\n  assert.equal(m.bandEnd, 100)\n  // peak sits at the clipped left edge (rank 1) and fades to zero by rank 5\n  assert.equal(m.stops[0].offset, 0)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[0].alpha, 0.88)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[m.stops.length - 1].offset, 100)\n  assert.equal(m.stops[m.stops.length - 1].alpha, 0)\n  const alphas = m.stops.map(s => s.alpha)\n  for (let i = 1; ${title}`)\n  }\n\n  const factor = await api.post('/ws/custproject-content/factor-create', {\n    data: {\n      project_id: projectId,\n      factor_title: 'Cost',\n      factor_description: 'Relative cost',\n      factor_polarity_positive: true,\n    },\n  })\n  await assertOk(factor, 'factor-create')\n\n  const shares = {} as Record<ShareAccessMode, SeededShare>\n  for (const mode of ALL_SHARE_ACCESS_MODES) {\n    const recipientEmail = `${uid('rcpt')}@example.com`\n    const password = mode.startsWith('password') ? uid('spw') : null\n    const body: Record<string, unknown> = {\n      shared_type: 'vote',\n      shared_entity_db_id: projectId,\n      access_mode: mode,\n      link_auto_send: false,\n      share_link_name: `${mode} ${uid('lnk')}`,\n      shared_with_person_name: 'E2E Recipient',\n      shared_with_email: recipientEmail,\n    }\n    if (password) body.share_password = password\n\n    const shareRes = await api.post('/ws/create-share-link', {\n      data: body,\n    })\n    await assertOk(shareRes, `create-share-link ${mode}`)\n    const shareBody = await shareRes.json()\n    if (shareBody.failure_reason) {\n      throw new Error(`share create ${mode} failed: ${shareBody.failure_reason}`)\n    }\n    const info = shareBody.link_info\n    shares[mode] = {\n      mode,\n      magicToken: info.magic_token,\n      shareId: info.id,\n      recipientEmail,\n      password,\n      projectTitle,\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    adminEmail,\n    adminPassword,\n    projectId,\n    projectTitle,\n    shares,\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function fetchLatestShareMagicKey(\n  api: APIRequestContext,\n  magicToken: string,\n): Promise<string> {\n  const res = await api.post('/ws/test-helper/get-latest-share-magic-key', {\n    data: { magic_token: magicToken },\n  })\n  await assertOk(res, 'get-latest-share-magic-key')\n  const body = await res.json()\n  if (!body.access_magic_key) {\n    throw new Error(`missing access_magic_key: ${JSON.stringify(body)}`)\n  }\n  return String(body.access_magic_key)\n}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":193,"totalLines":193,"truncated":false}},"title":"e2e/helpers/api.ts","time":{"start":1787327222567,"end":1787327223764}},"id":"prt_025011302001FYXohEF96Zh1aC","sessionID":"ses_fdb1eb3b9ffeI0StMLlOt9ncBe","messageID":"msg_02500ebde001QdLP0KhTsK4CzD"}}
